1a1(1): @#//gaN Di'i ka'i 'do zhuT so/
1b1(1): @##//rgya gar skad du/gaN DI sU tra/bod skad du/gaN DI'i mdo/sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan
1b2(1): 'das rgyal po'i khab 'od ma'i tshal bya ka lan da ka gnas pa na dge slong stong du tshang ba'i dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang /byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs chen po dang thabs cig tu bzhugs te/thams cad kyang dgra bcom
1b3(1): pa/zag pa zad pa/bya ba byas pa/byed pa byas pa/khur bor ba/bdag gi don rjes su thob pa/srid par sbyor ba yongs su zad pa/yang dag pa'i shes pas sems shin tu rnam par grol ba rnams dang /bcom ldan
1b4(1): 'das thabs cig go/de'i tshe rgyal po gsal rgyal zhes bya ba stan las langs nas bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der song ste phyin nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag btsal te/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad
1b5(1): ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das ma 'ongs pa'i dus su rtsod pa'i dus kyi tshe sems can thams cad sdig pa'i sems dang /'thab pa'i sems dang /'dod chags dang /zhe sdang dang /gti mug dang kun tu ldan pa/pha rol
1b6(1): gyi yul rnams la mchog tu zhen pa/gcig la gcig za ba la brtson pa/bcom ldan 'das kyis sngon gsungs pa'i dus 'jig par byed pa/dam pa'i chos spong bar byed pa/sangs rgyas dang /chos dang /'dod chags dang
1b7(1): bral ba rnams sun 'byin pa de rnams gzhig pa'i thabs gang gis byang chub la mchog tu gzhol bar 'gyur ba'i thabs bstan du gsol/de skad ces gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rgyal
1b8(1): po khyod sems can thams cad kyi don dang /phan pa dang /bde ba'i phyir dang /sangs rgyas dang /dge 'dun yang dag par bsrung ba'i phyir 'dri bar byed pa legs so legs so//de'i phyir legs par rab tu nyon la yid
1b9(1): la gzung zhig ngas bshad par bya'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das de'i tshe kun tu sgra sgrogs pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs so//bcom ldan 'das ting nge 'dzin gang la snyoms par bzhugs pa de'i tshe dmyal
2a1(1): @#//ba thams cad rgyun chad pa dang /sdang ba'i sems dang ldan pa thams cad kyang sdang ba'i sems dang bral zhing byang chub kyi sems la rab tu gzhol bar gyur pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/gsal 
2a2(1): rgyal khyod kyis mthong ngam/kun tu thos sam/shes sam/rtogs sam/bcom ldan 'das mthong lags so//bde bar gshegs pa mthong lags so//khums shing kun tu khums la 'tshal zhing rtogs so//bcom ldan 
2a3(1): 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//gsal rgyal 'di ni sems can thams cad kyi ma sangs rgyas dang /byang chub sems dpa' skyed pa/nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas dang /yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas thams cad kyis 
2a4(1): phyag byas pa/mchod pa/bsams pa/bsgoms pa/shes rab kyi rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa sgra'i gzugs kyis sems can thams cad yongs su skyong bar byed pa/mu stegs pa thams cad rnam par 'jig par byed pa/sdang 
2a5(1): ba'i sems 'dul bar byed pa/sdig pa'i sems 'joms par byed pa/zhe sdang ba'i sems rnam par sel bar byed pa/gti mug gi sems rab tu 'jig par byed pa/'dod chags kyi sems rnam par spong bar byed pa/
2a6(1): byang chub kyi sems yongs su sbyong bar byed pa/sang+yas kyi sems 'phel bar byed pa/sang+yas 'byung ba bsten par byed pa/'thab pa dang /'gyed pa dang /'khrug pa dang /rtsod pa dang /sdug bsngal ba dang /rmi lam ngan pa dang /kun 
2a7(1): 'dul ba thams cad 'jig pa/glo bur gyi nad dang /'chi ba zhi bar byed pa dang /rang dus ma yin par 'chi ba dang /rims la sogs pa zhi bar byed pa/pha rol gyi dpung 'dul bar byed pa/bdud rnam par 'jig par byedapa/gdung 
2a8(1): ba sel bar byed pa/skal ba bzang po yang dag par 'phel bar byed pa/tshe dang nad med pa ster bar byed pa/'chi ba'i 'jigs pa zhi bar byed pa/thar pa dang dpal 'phel bar byed pa/gang gis thos nas rjes su yi 
2a9(1): rang ba de la yid bzhin gyi nor bu ltar 'bras bu rab tu ster ba/yum shes rab kyi pha rab tu phyin pa gaN DI'i gzugs kyis bzhugs so zhes thos pa dang /gsal rgyal sems dang ba skyes nas yang bcom ldan 'das 
2b1(1): la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das 'di'i tshad ni ji tsam lags/mdog ni ci 'dra lags/bye brag ni du zhig lags/brdung ba ni ci lta ba lags/gang gis brdung bar bgyi/gang gis tshe gang gi dus na
2b2(1): ji srid du brdung bar bgyi/ji ltar gzhag par bgyi/de skad ces gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/'byor bar 'dod pas shing rnams ni/rab tu 'bad de brtag par bya/tsan+dan bil ba pa la sha/
2b3(1): a shwad tha dang tsan+dan dmar/rgya star ta la king shu ka/shing sha ba dang ta ma la/a mra star bu skyu ru ra//ting ti sar a shau ka/'di dag thams cad med na yang //'o se las ni 'bad de bya//rgyal po 'bad pas thams cad du//
2b4(1): 'di dag med na mi bya'o//tshad med shing ngan las byes dang //brdungs dang sgra ni mi 'byung ba'i//sangs rgyas yum 'di brdungs na ni//yul 'khor 'tshe ba nges par 'byung //gtsug lag khang du dge slong rnams//de bzhin blo ni mi mthun
2b5(1): dang //nye bar gdung ba 'byung 'gyur zhing //rnam pa du mar rtsod par 'gyur//mu stegs can gyi dbang 'gyur zhing //bdud kyang 'byung bar 'gyur ba yin//gdug pa'i klu ni du ma yis//'bru rnams kyang ni nyams par byed//rgyal khams
2b6(1): 'dir mang sems can rnams//rnam par 'jig pa nyid du 'gyur//gtsug lag khang yang 'jig 'gyur zhing //chos kyang nub par 'gyur ba yin//bdud kyi lha rnams dge slong gi/dge 'dun shin tu ma
2b7(1): bsdams byed//de ltar yang dag shes nas kyang //legs par yongs su brtags te bya//tshad ni tshung du sor brgyad kyi//brgyad cu rtsa bzhi nyid bya zhing //zheng du sor ni drug bya ste//'bangs su yang ni gnyis
2b8(1): bya'o//sor mo'i tshigs kyi tshad dag gis//zur bzhir tshad ni gnyis pa yin//legs mnyam nyid ni shin tu gsal//rtse gnyis sbal pa'i mgo bzhin no//de nas rdung bar byed pa dang //shing de nyid las bcad de bya//sor bcu gnyis
3a1(1): /kyi tshad dag la /bzlum zhing rtse gnyis ne'u 'i 'go' //chang zungs tshad kyang de bzhin te//shind 'jam dang lcibs mnyam bar //gang phyir de rdungs bzhag bkos pa//'og tu yang ni khri bya ba
3a2(1): gtsug lag khang gyi mgo steng ngam //yang na dbus ma'i steng du bya//de'i rab gnas bya ba ni //shind tu brtan ba'i chos kyi sku //skyed pa ma yid 'jig min //'dod nas ma skyes pa'i dge ba //de ltar
3a3(1): rab tu gnas byas nas//de ni spyan phyer gyurd pa yin //'gan de spyan dbye byas nas kyang //de'i 'og sdug lag khang kund tu //klu bdun gyis ni brgyand pa ltar //bskyed pa'i rim gyis brdung bar bya/
3a4(1): de 'og kon mchog gsum la ni//phyag byas nas ni 'du bya zhing //sha da dz+wa lan gsum brjod bas ni //chos kyi rnga rnams brdung bar bya //me tog mchod de de bzhin khrus //zan za nyi ma
3a5(1): shar nub che //dus gsum la ni lan gsum ste//sku khrus tshe lan gnyis sngags //glo bu gnod dang char myi 'bab //de bzhin 'thab pa dang rtsod pa dang //glog dang gnod pa chen po dang
3a6(1): gnas rtan rnams ni spyan 'dren dang //nad dang mu ge zhi ba'i phyir //ma chol da rims kyis rdung bar bya //dge slong rnams dang dge 'tshul gyis //dus rnams rtag tu brdungs pa ste//gso' sbyin la 
3a7(1): na gnas tshe'i//dge snyen gyis kyang brdung bar bya //mtshan nyid thams cad rdzogs pa dang //klu bdun gyis ni brgyand bar bya //rgyal po gang tshe yul khor du //'gan de'i sgra de sgrags kyudad pa//
3a8(1): der ni dpal dang grags pa dang //'dod pa dang ni lo legs 'gyur//pha rold dbung gyi 'jigs myed cing //'bru rnams kyis ni nyams myi 'gyur //sangs rgyas byin rlabs sbyong ba yis //der ni
3a9(1): bdud rnams 'dul bar 'gyur//chos kyi rnga ni srid rtser bcas //kun nas rab tu g.yo' bar 'gyur //dmyal dang yi dgas 'jig rten rnams /'jig rten 'gyur bar the tsom myed //thams cad
3a10(1): sangs rgyas rtag dang cang //thams cad chos la gzhol bar 'gyur//der ni dge 'dun 'dul bar ldan//mkhas pa rnams kyang 'phel bar 'gyur //'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug rnams der ni nub
3b1(1): bar 'gyur ba yin//rgyal po tshe ring chos ldan zhing /bde ba dgra dang ldan bar 'gyur //rgyal po thug lag khang kund tu //chos kyi rnga ni rdungs pa'i //sgra ni gang gyi rna ba'i //lam dag tu grags gyurd cing //
3b2(1): dus bzhi dag tu thos pa ni /de rnams byang chub thob par 'gyur //gang zhig snying nas yid rang ba de ni grol 'gyur the tsom myed//'gan 'de'i mdo' 'di lan cig thos //myi de skye bar dran bar 
3b3(1): 'gyur gang du mdo sde'i mchog 'di nas //yul 'khor de yang 'gyord par 'gyur //dus kun tu ni gnas pa dang //glog gam yang na mchod pa byed //'di ni yid la 'dzind byed pa sdig pa kun las grol
3b4(1): bar 'gyur //bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' bstsald pa dang //rgyal po gsal rgyal bcom ldan 'das la yang dang yang du phyag byas nas song ngo //bcom ldan 'das kyi de skad ces bka' bstsal nas
3b5(1): thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor de dag dang /lha dang myi dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang //dri zar bcas pa'i 'jig rten yir rangs ste//bcom ldan 'das kyisagasungs pa la mngon
3b6(1): bar stod do///'gan 'de'i mdo' rdzogs s+ho////rgya gar gyi mkhan po d+ha rma shi pad tra dang //lo tsha ba dge slong tshul khrims yon tan gyis
3b7(1): bsgyurd pa//zhu chen gyi lo tsha ba dge slong rin cen bzang pos btso sa te rtan la 'phab pa////sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo
3b8(1): 'di skad bdag gyis thos pa'i dus cig na//bcom ldan 'das yul sbyor byed nas//ljongs rgyu zhing gshegs pa na //grong bsIl bu can du byon to//grong sbyil bu can gyi grong gyi phyogs kyi shing gyi sha ba'i
3b9(1): tshal na//dge slong gyi dge 'dun chen po dang //thabs gcig tu zhugs so //de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis//dge slong rnams la bka' bstsald pa //dge dgong lang dgas ni tshul khrims yin no//
3b10(1): 'di dag ni ting nge 'dzind yin no//'di dag ni shes rab yin no//dge slong dag tshul khrims la goms bar byas bas//ting nge 'dzind la yun ring du gnas bar 'gyur ro /ting nge 'dzind la goms
4a1(1): //bar bya na//shes rab thob par 'gyur ro //shes rab la bsgoms bar byas nas //'di lta ste/'dod chags dang/zhe sdang dang/gti mug las sems yang dag pa kho nar ma dag pa rnam bar
4a2(1): grol bar 'gyur ro//de ltar sems yang dag pa kho na rnam bar 'grol ba'i //'phags pa nyan thos ni /bdag gyi zag pa ni zad do //tshangs bar spyod ba ni stend to//bya ba ni byas so //'di las
4a3(1): srid pa ni gzhan myi shes so //zhes yang dag pa pa kho nar shes bar 'gyur ro //bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' bstsald nas //dge slong de dag yid rang ste //bcom ldan 'das kyi 
4a4(1): gsungs pa la mngon par stod do//slab pa gsum gyi mdo' rdzogs s+ho////da ltar ni//go 'phang 'dus ma bgyis te /bdag gyis gong
4a5(1): bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsald pa //rab tu rnam bar phye ba'i chos rnams las /dam pa gang yin de ni rtog sa gyurd pa /de ni khyod kyis nyes bsams ma yin te //khyod kyis
4a6(1): nges pa legs bar rtogs pa yin /'od srung 'khor rnams skyo ba bskyed cig //de nas tshe dang ldan ba ldeng rgyas 'od srung bcom ldan 'das kyis skabs phye bas //ji ltar sems mnyam
4a7(1): bzhag pa bzhin du//bdag gyi bstan gyi steng na myi snang bar gyurd pa de lta bu'i ting nge 'dzind la snyoms par zhugs nas /shar phyogs kyi steng gyi nam mkha' la mngon bar 'phags te spyod lam rnam pa bzhi bo
4a8(1): 'di lta ste /chag pa dang /sdod pa dang /'dug pa dang /nyal ba dag byed do //me'i khams la yang snyoms par zhugs te /tshe dang ldan pa ltar rgyas 'od srung me'i khams la snyoms bar zhugs pa'i lus 
4a9(1): las 'od zer rnam pa sna tshogs /'di lta ste sngon po dag dang /ser po dag dang /dmar po dag dang /dkar po dag dang /gtsod ka dag dang /shel gyi kha dog lta bu byung ngo//cho 'phrul zung dag kyang rnam
4a10(1): bar bstand te /lus kyi smad nas me 'bar la /lus kyi stod nas chu grang mo'i rgyun 'bab pa dang /lus kyi stod nas me 'bar la /lusakyi smad nas chu grang mo'i rgyun 'thab pa //dag kyang rnam bar
4b1(1): bstand to //shar phyogs su lci ba bzhin du//lho dang nub dang/byang phyogs kyi steng gyi nam mkha' la yang mngon bar 'phags te //spyod lam rnam pa bzhi bo /'di lta ste chag pa dang /sdod pa dang /
4b2(1): nyal ba dag byed do//mye'i khams la yang snyoms bar zhugs te /tshe dang ldan ba ldeng rgyas 'od srung me'i khams la snyoms bar zhugs pa'i lus las 'od gzer   rnam pa sna tshogs //'di lta ste sngon bo dag dang
4b3(1): ser bo dag dang/dmar bo dag dang/dkar bo dag dang /btsod ka dag dang/shel gyi kha dog lta bu dag 'byung ngo //cho 'phrul zung dag kyang rnam bar bstand to//lus kyi smad nas 'o ma 'bar la/
4b4(1): lus kyi stod nas chu grang mo'i rgyun 'bab pa dang /lus kyi stod la me 'bar la /lus kyi smad nas chu grang mo'i rgyun 'thab pa dag kyang rnam bar bstan to //de ltar phyogs bzhir rdzu 'phrul gyi chom 
4b5(1): 'phrul rnam pa bzhi bstand nas //rdzu 'phrul mngon bar 'du bya ba de dag phyir bsdus te bla gos phrag pa gcig tu byas nas//pus mo g.yas pa'i lha pa sa btsugs nas //bcom ldan 
4b6(1): 'das ga la ba de logs (su)thal mo sbyar pa btud nas /bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsold to//bcom ldan 'das ni bdag gyi stond ba lags so //bdag ni bcom ldan 
4b7(1): 'das kyi nyan thos lags so//bcom ldan 'das ni bdag gyi stond pa lags so//stond pa lags so//bdag ni bcom ldan 'das kyi nyan thos lags so //nyan thos lags so//
4b8(1): bcom ldan kyis bka' bstsald pa //da 'od srung de bzhin no//de de bzhin te//'od srung ni khyod kyi stond pa yin no//khyod ni nga'i nyan thos yin no//'od srung ni khyod kyis stond pa yin no//
4b9(1): stond pa yin no//khyod mi nga'i nyan thos yin no //nyan thos yin no//'od s+r+rung khyod rang gyi stan gang yin ba la 'dug shig //de nas tshe dang ldan ba lteng rgyas 'od srung gyis bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la
4b10(1): mgo bos phyag 'tshal te/nang gi stan gang yin ba la 'dugago //de nas yul ma ga ta'i bram ze dang khyim (bdagi)rnams 'di snyam du sems te //dge sbyong chen po ni /ral pa can lteng rgyas 'od srung
5a1(1): @#//gyi drung na 'tshangs par spyad pa spyod pa ma yin gyi//ral pa can lteng rgyas 'od srung nyid//dge sbyong chen po'i spyan snga na tshangs par spyad pa spyod pa yin no//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis 
5a2(1): ka/yul ma ga ta'i rgyal po bzo sbyangs gzugs can snying bo la bka' bstsald pa /rgyal po chen po/gzugs ni skye ba yang yin/'jig pa yin te/de'i skye dang/'jig pa yang rtogs par rig par bya'o//
5a3(1): rgyal po chen po/tshor ba dang/'du shes dang/'du byed rnams dang/rnam par shes pa skye ba yang yin//'jig pa yang yin te/de'i skye ba dang/'jig pa yang rtog par bya'o//rgyal po chen po gzugs 
5a4(1): kyi skye ba dang/'jig pa'i chos nyid rtogs pa'i rigs kyi bus ni gzugs de yongs su shes pa yin no//rgyal po chen po/tshor pa dang 'du shes dang/'du byed rnalas dang rnam par shes pa'i
5a5(1): skye ba dang/'jig pa'i chosanyid rtogs pa'i rigs kyi bu ni rnam par shes pa de yongs su shes pa yin na//rgyal po chen po/gzugs yongs su shes pa'i rigs kyi bu ni/bdag dang bdag/
5a6(1): gi zhes bya ba khas lend par myi byed//nye bar lend par myi byed//lhag par gnas par myi byed/mngon par zhend par myi byed do//rgyal po chen po tshor ba dang/'du shes dang 'du byed rnams dang
5a7(1): rnam par shes pa yongs su shes pa'i rigs ki bu ni bdag dang bdag gyi zhes bya ba khas myi lend//nye bar len pa myi byed/lhag par gnas pa gnas par myi byed//mngon par zhend par myi phyed do//rgyal po
5a8(1): chen po gzugs bdag gyir myi byed//khas lend par myi byed /nye bar lend par myi byed //lhag par gnas par spyi byed/mngon par zhend bar myi byed pa'i rigs kyi bu 'am ni tshad myed pa dang/grangs myed
5a9(1): pa yin te//mya ngan las 'das pa yin par nga smra'o//rgyal po chen po/tshor ba dang/'du shes dang/'du byed rnams dang/rnam par shes pa na gang myi byed/khas len par myi byed/nye bar lend par
5a10(1): myi byed//lhag par gnas bar myi byed//mngon par zhand bar myi byed pa'i rigasakyi bu ni tshad myed pa dang//grangs myed pa rnams mya ngan las 'das pa yin bar nga smra'o//de nas yul ma ga ta'i

5b2(1): ma gyurd pa dang/byung bar myi 'gyur ba dang /da ltar yang myed pa ma yin zhing //gang de dang de dag tu dge ba dang sdig pa'i las ma byas ba yin/gang byas pa dag gyi rnam par smyind bar so sor myong bar byed pa dang/gang phung 
5b3(1): po 'di 'dor zhing phung po gzhan dang nying mtshams sbyor bar 'gyur ba'i bdag gam /sems can nam /srog gam/gang zag gam/skyes pa 'am/gso' ba 'am /shed las skyes sam/shed bu 'am /byed pa bo 'am
5b4(1): byed du 'jug pa bo 'am//skyed pa bo 'am /kun nas skyed pa bo 'am //slong ba bo 'am //kun nas slong ba po 'am/smra ba po 'am/myong ba bo 'am /so sor myong ba bo de gang zhig yin no snyam mo //
5b5(1): de rnams bcom ldan 'das kyi yul ma ga ta'i bram ze dang khyim bdag rnams kyi sems thugs kyis mkhyend nas //dge slong rnams la bka' bstsald pa /dge slong dag byis pa so so'i skye bo thos pa dang/
5b6(1): myi ldan na ni //bdag gam bdag ces tha snyad 'dogs pa rjes su lhung ste//de la ni bdag gam bdag gir bya ba myed do//dge slong dag /sdug bsngal 'di skye ba ni yang skye la /sdug bsngal 
5b7(1): 'gag pa na yang 'gag go//'du byed rnams skye ba na yang skye la 'du byed rnams 'gag pa na yang 'gag ste/rgyu de dag dang rkyen de dag la rten nas /sems can rnams kyi 'du byed kyi rgyun byung
5b8(1): bar 'gyur te/dge slong dag /de bzhin gshegs pa ni /'du byed kyi rgyun nyid mtshams sbyor bar thugs chud nas //sems can rnams kyi 'chi 'pho ba dang skye ba dang dogs par mdzad do//dge slong dag/
5b9(1): ngas lha'i spyan rnam par dag pa myi las shind tu 'das pas sems can 'chi 'pho ba dag dang /skye ba dag dang/gzugs bzang ba dag dang/mdog rngan pa dag dang/dman pa dag dang /gya nom pa dag dang/
5b10(1): bde 'gror 'gro ba dag dang/ngan 'gror 'gro ba dag kyang gzigs la //sems can shes pa dang/ldan pa 'di dag ni lus kyis nyes par spyod pa dang ldan pa//ngag dang yid kyis nyes par spyod pa dngaldan pa /
6a1(1): //'phags pa rnams la bskurd pa 'debs pa /log par lta ba dag ste //log par lta ba'i las dang/chos yang dag par blangs pa'i rgyus /lus zhig ste shi ba'i 'og tu rgyu de dang /rkyen des ngar song
6a2(1): ngan 'gro log par ltung ba'i sems can dmyal ba rnams kyi nang du skye bar 'gyur ba dang/sems can shes pa dang ldan pa 'di dag ni lus kyi legs par spyod pa dang ldan pa /ngag dang yid kyi legs par spyod pa dang
6a3(1): 'phags pa rnams la skurd pa myi 'debs pa /yang dag par lta ba dag ste /yang dag par lta ba'i las dang chos yang dag par blangs ba'i rgyus lus zhig nas shi ba'i 'og tu rgyu de dag rkyen des bde 'gro mtho ris kyi 'jig
6a4(1): rten gyi lha rnams kyi nang du skye bar gyur ro //zhes sems can rnams las ji lta ba bzhin du nye bar 'gro ba dag kyang yang dag pa ci lta ba bzhin du rab tu mkhyend to//dge slong dag /des de ltar mkhyend cing
6a5(1): de ltar gzigs pas na /nga ni gang nas kyang byung bar ma gyurd pa dang /byung bar myi 'gyur ba dang /da ltar yang myed pa ma yin zhing /gang de dang de ga tu dge ba dang sdig pa'i las byas shing
6a6(1): byas pa dag gyi rnam par smyind pa so sor myed par byed pa dang /gang phung po 'di 'dod zhing phung po gzhan dang nying tsham sa sbyor ba'i chos kyi brda ma rtogs par /bdag gam/sems

10b6(1): bgyi ba yang mchis lags so//nub phyogs na grong khyer gtsang ma gasra ldan dang//gos can dang//gos chog can dang /pad ma can dang //pad ma bzang po can dang //sa gzhi can
10b7(1): zhes bgyi ba dag kyang mchis lags so //chab rgyun mang bo'i gnas gyurdpa //'dzind pa zhes bgyi 'i mtsho'i mchis te//chab rgyun tha dad de las byung //de las dgur chang 'bab bar 'gyur 
10b8(1): btsun ba grong khyer u shi ra'i sbubs can gyi pha rold na//lus gnas dang ni gnas yangs dang //de pas gnas yangs rgyun lugs can //rgyun lugs mang dang de bas mang //bar snang kha la bya ltar gda'
10b9(1): btsun ba grong khyer sbubs can gyi pha roladabyang phyogs na //'dun sa bya dgar cang zhes bgyi ba de mchis te//der ni gnod sbyin rnams 'du zhing //gnas lags te //rkang myi sdug la su 'phyangs dang//
10b10(1): 'jigs dung lto sbu can dag mchis //de ljon shin 'bras bu brtag //rma bya khyung khrung sgra snyan dang//khu byug sgra skad che na bo dang //shang shang de'u dag sgra byind cing //rma bya rnams kyi sgra dag dang //ko'u kus
11a1(1): //bA dang chun lhag dang//bya rnams rnam pa sna tshogs skad byind cing //me tog sbrang phu'i ro la dga' //ku be ra de klu gar gyis //pad can lhan cig 'phe//btsun pa grong khyer lcang lo can gyi pha 
11a2(1): dold na//rgyal po dpa' bo zhes bgyi ba yul 'khor mu khyud na //mchis lags so//bdag gang na mchis pa'i mdun sa de ni khyu mchog can zhes bgyi ste//bdag gyi mying rnam thos kyi bu zhes bgyi ba lags so//
11a3(1): de la gsang sngags kyi tshig ni 'di dag lags so//tad yA tha //I lI myi lA+e//si li ma le//ki Ta Ti mU le //hi li ka Ta Ti mU le//hu du mA+e //hu dur tum me //go 'u ri gan d+hA ri//go do ha na /
11a4(1): o ke mo ke sA ha////btsun pa bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar bdag gis dri za rnams dang //dri za'i sde dpon rnams dang //dmag dpon rnams
11a5(1): kyi mying brjod par bgyi lags so//'di lta ste 'dod myos dga' bas myos pa dang//klu'i myos pa spun dang ni //'phreng ba sa myos dang //sbrang rtsi myos//me tog myos pa spun rnams dang
11a6(1): rtag tu myos dang dpal gyis myos //nor gyis myos dang dga' byed dang //mud pa la pad ma zla ba dang//lda 'dzind dang ni ne tso'i sgra//tum bu ru dang gtsug lnga dang//gser spyod bu mo ser can
11a7(1): pa bzangs dang phur bu dang//'gro na ci snyed rgyal po lags //de dag ni bdag gyi spun lags te//de dag bdag la 'dri za 'di ni//bdag la tshe'o//dri za 'di ni bdag myi gtong ngo zhes mchi zhing //
11a8(1): de dag la go bar bgyid lags so//bdag la tshe ba'i dri za dang//bdag la myi gtong ba'i dri za 'is//legs par smras pa 'di thos nas //ma lnga'i dog pa bzhin//de'i glad pa mdun du 'gas//
11a9(1): de la gsang sngags kyi tshig 'di dag lags so//tad yA thA //kum tu le'u kun tu le /kum tu le'u kun tu le /e hi me hi/sa dA mad d+hi ki/sa da b+ha Na Ta ni ke /ti Sha tu Sha pad do swa ha //btsun pa bcom 
11a10(1): ldan 'das nyan thos kyi dge slong 'am //dge slong ma 'am//dge snyen ma 'am /dge snyen ma 'am la la kun tu rgyu ba dang kun tu rgyu ba ma lags pa dang mthun pa'i mdo//rigs sngags dang spung ba dang
11b1(1): sug pa rkang pa la phyag 'tshal IIIIIIIIIIIIIO /rgya cher lung mnos shing //'don par bgyi ba de la //gal te dri za 'am //dri za mo 'am /dri za rgan po 'am/dri za'i bu pho 'am //dri za'i bu mo 'am 
11b2(1): dri za'i 'khor bo 'am //dri za'i 'khor mo 'am //dri za'i mngag gzhug pa 'am //dri za'i mngag gzhug pa tshe bar bgyid pa ////glags lta zhing glags tshol ba la la zhig /nye bar mchi zhing //nye bar 'greng 'am
11b3(1): nye bar mchil bar gyurd na /glags myi rnyed cing //dmyigs pa myi rnyed la /dri za rnams kyi (dri)za (dri) za (dri)'du ba'i nang du /mchir myi chud cing //rgyal bo'i pho brang lcang lo can du yang gnas myi thob ste//de'i glad pa ldum
11b4(1): bu a rdza ka'i mdog pa bzhin //'tshalda ba bdun du 'gas lags so//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das kyi nyan thos kyi dge slong ngam//dge slong ma 'am//dge snyen nas dge snyen ma'i bar du gang la la//
11b5(1): kun tu rgyu ba dang kun tu rgyu ba ma lags pa dang mthun pa'i mdo//rigs sngags dang srung ba dang //sug pa rkang pa la phyag 'tshal bar 'gyur ba 'di nyid rgya cher lung mnos shing khra rgond bar bgyid pa de la //
11b6(1): gal te sha za 'am /sha za mo 'am /sha za rgad po 'am /sha za rgan mo 'am //sha za bu pho 'am /sha za bu mo 'am /sha za'i 'khor pho 'am//sha za' 'khor mo 'am /sha za'i mngag gzhug
11b7(1): pa 'am//sha za'i mngag gzhug ma 'am /'tshe bar bgyi ba ////glags lta zhing //glags tshol ba la la zhig /nye bar 'chi zhing /nye bar gleng ngam //nye bar mchis bar 'gyur na /glags
11b8(1): myi rnyed cing //sha za rnams kyis (shing)za I'du ba'i nang du yang mchir myi chud cing //rgyal bo'i pho brang lcang lo can du yang gnas myi thob cing /de'i glad pa ldum bu a rdza ka'i mdog pa bzhin 'tshal ba bdun du 'gas 
11b9(1): lags so//tad yA thA /hu mA+e hu mA+e /hu me hu me me lA ba ti ye swa ha ////btsun pa bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar bdag gyi grul bum rnams dang //grul bum chen po rnams dang /grul bum 
11b10(1): gyi sde dpon rnams dang /dmag dpon rnams kyi mying brjod bar bgyid lags so//'di lta ste byin dang te byin kri myi sha //rtsibs logs che dang sus po che /lag chu dang ni sa la rgyu //g.yon phyogs nag po 
12a1(1): /dmar po dang /srin mdog dang ni dri myed dang /rna ba ring dang 'brug sgra sgrogs //ral pa can dang stobs bcu dang //srid sgrub dang ni 'dod dgur mchi//'jog po dang ni bya ba sbed /la ba bzang 
12a2(1): ldan gsum po dang //tshon kun dang ni dmyigs kra po//'gro gnas ci snyed mchis pa lags //de dag ni bdag gyi spun lags te //de dag bdag grul bum 'di ni bdag la tshe'o//grul bum 'di ni bdag myi
12a3(1): gtong ngo //zhes mchi zhing de dag la go bar bgyid pa lags so//bdag la che ba'i grul bum dang /bdag myi gtong ba'i grul bum gyis //legs par smras pa 'di 'thos na//ma rdza ka'i mdog pa bzhin //
12a4(1): de'i glad pa bdun du 'gas //de la gsang sngags kyi tshig ni 'di dag lags so//tad ya thA /si re bi sa re /si ri bi sa ri /si ri bi sa re /kri Sh+Na tsha ka li /ping li swa na ////
12a5(1): btsun pa bcom ldan 'das kyi nyan thos dge slong dam//dge slong ma /'am //dge snyen nam/dge snyen ma gang la la //kun tu rgyu ba dang /kun tu rgyu ba ma yin pa dang mthun pa'i mdo//
12a6(1): rigs sngags dang srung ba dang sug pa rkang pa la 'tshal bar 'gyur ba 'di nyid rgya cher khung mnos shing 'dond bar bgyid pa de la gal te grul 'bum 'am /grul bum mo 'am /grul bum rgan po 'am /grul bum rganamo 'am 
12a7(1): grul bum gyi bu pho 'am /grul bum gyi bu mo 'am /grul bum gyi 'khor mo 'am /grul bum gyi mdag gzhug pa 'am //grul bum gyi mngag gzhug ma tshe 'bar bgyid pa //glags lta zhing glags tshol ba la la zhig //
12a8(1): nye bar 'chi zhing /nye bar 'greng 'am//nye bar mchis bar gyurd na /glags myi rnyed cing //dmyigs pa myi rnyed la /grul bum rnams kyi grul bum 'du ba'i nang du mchir myi chud cing //rgyal bo'i pho brang lcang lo can 
12a9(1): du yang gnas myi thos ste //de'i glad pa ldum bu a rdza ka 'i gdog pa bzhin tshal ba mdun du gas lags so//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das kyi nyan thos dge slong ngam /dge slong ma 'am//dge sten nam//
12a10(1): dge snyen ma gang la la //kun tu rgyu ba kun tu rgyu ba ma lags pa dang /mthun pa'i mdo //rigs sngags dang srung ba dang /sug pa rkang pa la phyag 'tshal bar 'gyur ba 'di nyid /rgya cher lung mnos shing 
12b1(1): 'dond bar bgyid pa 'di la/gal te yi dgas sam /yi dgas mo'i yi dgas rgan bo 'am //yi dgas rgan mo 'am /yi dgas kyi bu pho 'am //yi dgas kyi bu mo 'am /yi dgas kyi 'khor lo 'am /yi dgas
12b2(1): kyi m+ngag gzhug pha 'am//yi dgas kyi m+ngag gzhug ma 'am//tshe bar bgyid pa 'am //glags lta zhing glags tshol ba la la zhig //nye bar 'chi zhing //nye bar 'greng 'am//nye bar mchis bar gyurd na //glags
12b3(1): myi rnyed cing /dmyigs pa myi rnyed la /yi dgas rnams kyi yi dgas 'dru ba 'i nang du mchir myi rgud cing //rgyal bo pho brang ljang lo can du yang gnas myi thob ste//de'i glad pa ldum bu a rdza ka'i mdog pa bzhin
12b4(1): 'tshal ba bdun du 'gas lags so//de la sngags kyi tshig ni 'di dag lags so//tad yA thA/yu mA+e yu mA+e /yu me le /yu mi lA //ba ti ye swA ha //btsun ba bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar /bdag gyis klu
12b5(1): rnams dang //klu chen bo rnams dang /klu'i sde dpon rnams dang /dmag dpon rnams kyi myi brjod par bgyid lags so//'di lta ste /dga' dang nye dga' kun dga' dang //nor rgyas dang ni
12b6(1): 'dzam bu bzang /ser po dang ni zho rna dang /dbang bo'i lag ba dang /bkra shis dang /mya ngan myed dang mya ngan bral rab 'grol dang ni rgyal ba'i bdag //chos yangs po dang ngos bkra dang /
12b7(1): yangs pa can dang dgra bdud rgyal//gdengs kha che dang /skar mdog dang /nag bo che dang lag 'gro dang //ra skyong dang ni 'jog bo dang //g.yon phyogs de bzhin gnag po dang //sngo bsangs
12b8(1): dkar dang /'grogs pa ni //'gro gnas ci snyed mchis pa lags //de dag ni bdag gyi spun lags ste //de dag la bdag glu 'di ni bdag la 'chi'o//klu 'di ni bdag myi gtong ngo //zhes mchi zhing
12b9(1): de dag la go bar bgyid lags so//bdag lA tshe ba'i klu rnams dang //bdag myi gtong ba'i klu rnams kyis legs//bar smras pa 'di thos na //a rdza ka'i mdog pa bzhin//de'i glad pa bdun du
12b10(1): 'gas//de la gsang sngags kyi tshig ni 'di dag lags so//tad y+ya tha/tsi le bi tsi le /tsi le bi tsi le /go'u ri gan d+ha ri//mAn d+ha gi tsan Ta li swA ha////btsun ba bcom ldan 'das
13a1(1): //kyi nyan thos dge slong ngam//dge slong ma 'am/dge snyen nam//dge snyen ma gang la la//kun tu rgyu rnang/kun tu rgyu ba ma lags pa mthun pa'i mdo//rigs sngags dang srung ba dang//sdug pa

13a7(1): shing 'dond par bgyid pa 'di la//gal te lus srul bo 'am//lus srul mo'am//lus srul rgad po 'am//lus srul rgan mo 'am//lus srul bo'i bu pho 'am//lus srul bo bu mo 'am//lus srul
13a8(1): bo'i 'khor pho 'am//lus srul po'i 'khor mo 'am//lus srul po'i mngag gzhug pa 'am//lus srul bo'i mngag gzhug ma//'tshe bar bgyid pa glags lta zhing glags tshol ba la la zhig//nye bar mchi zhing
13a9(1): nye bar greng 'am//nye bar mchis par gyurd na//glags myi rnyed//cing dmyigs pa myi rnyed la//lus srul bo rnams kyi lus srul po 'du ba'i nang du mchir myi chud cing//rgyal po 'i pho brang lcang lo can du

13b1(1): pa ha na pa t+yA ya swA hA ////btsun pa bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar bdag gyis gnod sbyin rnams dang//gnod sbyin chen po rnams dang/gnod sbyin gyi sde dpon rnams dang//dmag dpon rnams kyi mying 
13b2(1): brjod bar bgyid lags so//'di lta ste/dbang po zla chu lha dang//pa ra da dza skye dgu'i bdag/dbang ldan mtshan ldan 'dod gtso dang/mgrin ngan dang ni mgul ma nges dang//stobs ldan nor bu nor spyod dang//
13b3(1): sgra rab dang ni nye ldan dang ri mnyam gser can gang bo dang//seng ldeng ba dang g.yon can dang//gnod sbyin pa 'khor gnas dang/rgyal pa khyu mchog myi'i rgyal po//dri za sna tshogs sde dang ni/lcags mdung ring dang/
13b4(1): ma ldan dang//tshigs lnga ser po yid dga' dang//gnod sbyin ring po 'khord dang bcas//'bras bu gsum dang mgul gsum pa//'gro gnas ji snyed mchis pa lags//de dag ni bdag gyi spun
13b5(1): lags so//de dag la bdag gnod sbyin 'di ni bdag la tshe'o//gnod sbyin 'di ni bdag myi gtong ngo//zhes mchi zhing//de dag la bdag go bar bgyid lags so//bdag la tshe ba'i
13b6(1): gnod sbyin dang//bdag myi gtong ba'i gnod sbyin gyis//legs bar smras pa 'di thos te//a rdza ka'i gdog pa bzhin/de'i glad pa bdun du 'gas//de la gsang sngags
13b7(1): kyi tshig ni 'di dag lags so//tad y+ya tha//hi li myi le//hi li myi le/ki rti mu le/o tum be tum be/o tum ba tum be/o g+ge mo g+ge/go ho de/b+hing ga re/ri shi sar te me swA hA

13b9(1): dang srung ba dang/sug pa rkang pa la phyag 'tshal bar 'gyur bar 'di nyid//rgya cher lung mnos shing 'dond par bgyid pa de la//gal te gnod sbyin nam/gnod sbyin mo 'am/gnod sbyin rgan po 'am

14a1(1): /gyi mngag gzhug pa 'am/tshe bar bgyid pa /glags lta zhing glags tshol ba la la zhig/nye bar mchi zhing /nye bar 'greng 'am/nye bar gyurd pa mchis bar gyurd na //glags myi rnyed cing /dmyigs pa
14a2(1): myi rnyed la /gnod sbyin rnams kyi gnod sbyin 'du ba'i nang du mchir myi chud cing /rgyal po'i pho brang lcang lo can gyi gnas myi thob ste//de'i glad pa ldum bu a rdza ka'i gdog pa bzhin 'tshal ba bdun du 'gas lags so//
14a3(1): btsun ba bcom ldan 'das kyis nyan thos kyi dge slong 'am//dge slong ma 'am //dge snyen nam /dge snyen ma gang la la /kun tu rgyu ba dang /kun tu rgyu ba ma lags pa dang /mthun ba'i mdo' /rigs sngags
14a4(1): dang srung ba dang /sug pa rkang pa la phyag 'tshal bar 'gyur ba 'di nyid //rgya cher lung mnos shing 'dond bar bgyid pa de la //gal te srin pho 'am//srin mo 'am/srin po rgan po 'am /srin po rgan mo 'am
14a5(1): srin po'i bu pho 'am /srin po'i bu mo 'am /srin po'i 'khor pho 'am /srin po'i 'khor mo 'am /srin po'i mngag gzhug pa 'am//srin po'i mngag gzhug ma 'am//tshe bar bgyid pa /glags
14a6(1): lta zhing glags 'tshol ba la la zhig /nye bar mchi zhing /nye bar 'greng 'am//nye bar mchis bar gyurd na /glags myi rnyed cing /dmyigs pa myi rnyed na /srin po rnams kyi
14a7(1): srin po 'du ba'i nang du mchir myi chud cing /rgyal po'i pho brang lcang lo can du yang gnas myi thob ste //de'i glad pa ldum bu a rdza ka'i gdog pa bzhin /tshald pa bdun du 'gas lags so//de la gsang
14a8(1): sngags kyi tshig ni 'di dag lags so//tad d+ya thA /hi li hi li /hu lu hu lu /yu me yu mI li /yu mi la ba dri ye swa hA//de nas rgyal bo chen po rnam thos kyi bus //bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan
14a9(1): sngar kun tu rgyu ba dang kun tu rgyu ba ma yin ba dang mthun pa'i mdo'//rigs sngags dang srung ba dang /sug pa rkang pa la phyag 'tshal bar 'gyur ba 'di nyid /rgya cher bshad nas//bcom ldan 'das skye zhabs
14a10(1): la mgo bos phyag tshal te //de nyid du myi snang bar gyurd to//de nas bcom ldan 'das de nyid kyi mtshan mo 'das pa dang //dge slong gyi dge 'dun gyi ba dan bshams pa de nyid la gzhugs so//bzhugs
14a11(1): nas kyang bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong rnams la bka' bstsald pa //dge slong dag /ma dang rgyal po chen po rnam thos kyi bu kha dog 'phags pa/'khor gnod sbyin du ma dang /'khor gnod sbyin du la dang
14b1(1): 'khor gnod sbyin brgya phrag du ma dang //'khor gnod sbyin stong phrag du ma dang /'khor gnod sbyin 'bum phrag du ma dang ldan ba /nam sros pa dang nga ga la ba der 'ongs ste phyind nas /nga'i zhabs la
14b2(1): 'go' bos phyag 'tshal te /phyogs gcig tu 'dug go//de'i tshe rgyal bo rnam thos kyi bu'i kha dog gyi mthus rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi 'tshal thams cad snang ba chen pos gsald bar gyurd to//rgyal po rnam thos
14b3(1): kyi bu phyogs cig tu 'dug nas /de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gsold to//rkang gnyis dam pa rdzogs sangs rgyas //blo ldan khyod kyis gar mkhyend pa //lha rnams kyis ni de ma 'tshald//
14b4(1): dpa' chen khyod la phyag 'tshal lo//sangs rgyas gang dag 'das pa dang //da ltar byung dang ma byon dang //thams cad la ni bdag phyag 'tshal //khyod la 'ang bdag ni skyabs su mchi //btsun pa bcom ldan
14b5(1): 'das kyi nyan thos kyi dge slong ngam //dge slong ma dang /dge snyen dang /dge snyen ma gang dag //gnas mal 'gon pa dag dang /nags 'dab dag dang /bas mtha' dag na gnas pa dag kyang
14b6(1): mchis la//de na gnod sbyin dang myi ma lags pa gdug pa nyug mar gnas pa gang dag //bcom ldan 'das kyi gsung rab la mngon bar dad pa dag dang /mngon bar ma dad pa dag kyang chis te
14b7(1): gnod sbyin dang myi ma lagasapa gdug pa rtag mar gnas pa gang dag/bcom ldan 'das kyis gsung rab la mngon par ma dad pa de dag ni ches mang lags ste/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das
14b8(1): kyi nyan thos dge slong rnams dang/dge slong ma rnams dang /dge snyen rnams dang /dge snyen ma rnams 'di ltar bdag nyid bsrung ba dang/skyab pa dang /bde' ba la reg bar gnas par bgyi ba dang /gnod
14b9(1): sbyin dang myi ma lags pa gtug pa nyug mar gnas pa mngon par ma dad pa dag /mngon bar dad par bgyi ba dang /mngon bar dad pa rnams kyang slar zhing 'phel bar bgyi ba'i slad du //kun tu rgyu ba dang kun
14b10(1): tu rgyu ba ma lags pa dang mthun pa'i mdo//rigs sngags dang bsrung ba dang /sug pa rkang pa la phyag 'tshal bar 'gyur ba 'di nyid /rgya cher 'dzind cing kun chub bar bgyid na legs so legs so//
15a1(1): //zhes gsold pa dang/dge slong dag ngas rgyal po chen po rnam thos kyi bu la cang myi gsung bas gnang bar mdzad do //de nas rgyal bo rgyal po chen po rnam thos ki bu//ngas cad myi gsung pas gnang
15a2(1): bar rig nas /nga'i zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshald te //nga'i spyan sdang kun tu rgyu ba dang rgyu ba ma yin ba dang mthun pa'i mdo //rigs sngags dang bsrung ba dang /sug pa rkang pa la phyag 'tshal bar
15a3(1): 'gyur pa 'di nyid /rgya cher gsol to //skye bo gang dag gtong phod cing //'phra ma myi mchi ser sna 'bral //gang dag sangs rgyas skyabs mchis pa //myi de rnams kyi rnyed pa lags//gang gyi chad la
15a4(1): rtend nas ni//des na nyin mor brjod pa'i //nyi ma chen po dkyil 'khor can //nyi gdugs gang nas 'chang 'gyur ba'i //phyogs de mngon bar skyong bgyid pa //rgyal chen grags dang ldan
15a5(1): ba ni//dri za'i bdag po rgyal po ste //mying ni yul 'khor srung zhe /sa bgyi //de ni nyi du lhan cig tu //klu dang gar gyis //rtag tu rtse//dri za rnams kyis yongs bskord cing //dri zas
15a6(1): mdun gyis bltas pa lags de la bu ni mang mchis te//brgya bcu dang ni bcu dang cig //de dag mying mthun grags pa ni //dbang po zhes bgyi bstobs po che //lha dang lha myin g.yul 'gyed pa //
15a7(1): kun tu gzhan gyis mying /thub lags//de dag rnams kyis rdzogs sangs rgyas //sangs rgyas nyi ma'i mnyen mthong nas /myi yis phyag bgyis khyod la ni//skyes bu cang shes khyod la 'dud //skyes mchog
15a8(1): khyod la phyag tshal zhes //rgyang ring gnas shing phyag kyang 'tshal //bdag la dge bas gzigs su gsol //khyod la myi yin phyag 'tshal ba//de skad yang dang yang du thos //de slad bdag ni 'di skad ces
15a9(1): 'drend pa rgyal la phyag 'tshol cig //ko'u ta ma rgyal la phyag 'tshol cig//sangs rgyas rig dang zhabs ldan ba //mya ngan 'das la phyag tshol mchi //de ltar shar gyi phyogs de ni//bzang ba lags shes skye
15a10(1): bo mchi//phyogs de mngon bar skyong bgyid pa//rgyal chen grags dang ldan ba lags//gang na yi dgas zhes bgyi ba //rgyab kyi sha 'tshal phra ma can //srog gcod bgyid pa ma rungs pa //
15b1(1): 'jungs shing myi snyan smra bgyid pa'i //phyogs de mngon bar skyong bgyid pa'i//rgyal chen grags dang ldan pa ni//grul bum bdag po rgyal po ste //mying ni 'phags skyes bo zhes bgyi//de ni nye ru lhan 
15b2(1): cig tu//glu dang gar gyis (྾)rtag tu rtse//grul bum rnams kyis yongs bskord cing //grul bum bdun gyis bltas pa lags //de la bu ni mang mchis (྾)ste//brgyad bcu dang ni bcu (྾)gcig //de dag mying
15b3(1): mthun grags pa ni //dbang bo zhes bgyi ba'i stobs po che //lha dang lha myin g.yul 'gyed pa //kun tu gzhan gyis myi thub lags de dag rnams kyis sardzogs sangs rgyas//sangs rgyas nyi ma'i mnyen mthong nas
15b4(1): myi yis phyag bgyis khyod la ni//skyes bu cang shes khyod la 'dud //skyes mchog khyod la phyag 'tshal zhes //rgyang ring gnas shing phyag kyang 'tshal //bdag la dge bas gzigs su gsol //khyod la myi
15b5(1): mying phyag 'tshal ba//de skad yang dang yang du thos//de slad bdag ni 'di skad ces //'dren pa rgyal la phyag tshol cig //ko'u ta ma brgya la phyag tshol cig //sangs rgyas rig dang zhabs 
15b6(1): ldan pa //mya ngan 'das la phyag tshol mchi //de ltar lha'i phyogs de ni //bzang ba lags shes skye bo mchi //phyogs de mngon bar skyong bgyid pa //rgyal chen grags dang ldan pa lags
15b7(1): gang zhig nub ba kho na las//des na mtshan mo brjod pa'i //nyi ma chen po dkyil 'khor can /nyi gdugs gang tu nub 'gyur ba //de na mtsho chen zab mo ni //shind tu 'da' bar dka' ba ste
15b8(1): de ni chu sngon rgya mtsho zhes //skye bos rtogs pa ma mchis pa'i //phyogs de mngon bar skyong bgyed pa //rgyal chen grags (྾)dang ldan pa ni //klu'i bdag bo rgyal bo ste //mying ni myig myi bzang 
15b9(1): zhes bgyi //de ni nye du lhan cig tu //glu dang (྾)gyis rtag tu rtse//klu rnams kyis ni yongs bskord cing //klu Imdun gyis bltas pa lags //de la bu ni mang mchis te //brgya bcu dang bcu dang cig
15b10(1): de dag myi mthun grags ba ni//dpa' bo zhes bgyi stobs bo che //lha dang lha myin g.yul 'gyed pa //kun tu gzhan gyis myi thub lags //de dag rnams kyis rdzogs sangs rgyas //sangs rgyas nyi I
16a1(1): //mnyen mthong nas //myi yis phyag bgyis khyod la ni//skyes bu cang shes khyod la 'dud//skyes mchog khyod la phyag 'tshal zhes //rgyang (na)gnas shing phyag kyang 'tshal //bdag la dge ba (gsang dang dpe mgu ba )gzig(sa ma ba )
16a2(1): su gsol//khyod la myi myin phyag 'tshal ba //de skad yang dang yang du thos de skad bdag ni 'di skad ces //'drend pa brgya la phyag 'tshol cig//ko'u ta ma brgya la phyag 'tshol cig //sangs rgyas rig dang
16a3(1): zhabs ldan ba /mya ngan 'das la phyag 'tshal mchi//de ltar nub kyi phyogs de ni //bzang ba lags shes skye bo mchi //phyogs de mngon par skyong bgyid pa//rgyal chen grags dang ldan pa lags //
16a4(1): gang na byang gyi sgra myi snyan //dga' zhing lhun cen lta mdzes dang/gang na myi rnams tshe lo ni //brgya phrag bcu pa skye 'gyur zhing //de dag myi rmo myi 'debs la//(gsang dang dpe mgu ) //dri dang ro dang ldan ba dang //dri gda' me tog tshogs 'dra zhing /
16a5(1): bgyid cing //tshe ring mchog dang ldan ba dang /nges par yongs su spyod bgyid cing //zas su sa lu kha dog dang //dri dang ro dang ldan ba dang//dri gda' me tog tshog 'dra zhing /
16a6(1): lha'i bdud rtsi 'dra ba'i tshal //na li ke ra bskol bgyis nas //tshod mar de 'tshal sran tshod myin //gang na me lce rtse 'dra ba'i//sman dag khrung bar 'gyur ba'i //phyogs de mngon bar skyong bgyid pa
16a7(1): rgyal chen grags dang ldan ba ni //gnod sbyin bdag po rgyal po ste//mying ni ku pe ra zhes bgyi //de ni he du lhan cig tu //glu dang gar gyis rtag (tu )rtse //gnod sbyin rnams kyis yongs bskord cing
16a8(1): gnod sbyin mdun gyis bltas pa lags //de la bu ni mang mchis te //brgya bcu dang ni bcu dang gcig //de dag mying mthun grags pa ni//dbang po Izhes bgyi stobs po che//lha dang lha myin 
16a9(1): g.yul 'gyed pa//kun tu gzhan gyis myi thub lags //de dag rnams kyis rdzogs sangs rgyas //sangs rgyas nyi ma'i gnyen mthong nas //myi yis phyag bgyis khyod la ni //skyes bu cang shes khyod la
16a10(1): 'dud//skyes mchog khyod la phyag 'tshal zhes//rgyang ring gnas shing phyag kyang 'tshal //bdag la dge bas gzigs su gsol//khyod la myi myin phyag 'tshal ba//de skad yang dang yang du thos //
16b1(1): de slad bdag ni 'di skad ces //'drend pa brgya la phyag tshol cig //ko 'u ta ma brgya la phyag 'tshol cig//sangs rgyas rig dang zhabs ldan pa //mya ngan 'das la phyag 'tshal mchi//de ltar byang gyi
16b2(1): phyogs deni//bzang ba lags shes skye bo mchi//phyogs de mngon bar skyong bgyid pa //rgyal chen grags dang ldan ba lags//shar phyogs na ni yul 'khor srung //lho phyogs na ni 'ang gas sh+yeso//nub phyogs
16b3(1): na ni myig myi bzang //byang phyogs na ni ku be ra /rgyal po chen po de bzhi dag //'jig rten skyong ba grags dang ldan //rdzu 'phrul chen po stobs dang ldan //phyogs bzhi dag ni skyong bar gyid //rgyal chen de dag
16b4(1): gyi mngag gzhug pa //gnod sbyin 'dod dgur mchis pa rnams /de dag glang po che 'tshal zhing //phyogs dag tu rnam bar rgyu /de dag ni chibs rta dang //rnga mo dang /khyu mchog dang /ma he dang /ra dang
16b5(1): lug dang sbrul dang /bya dang /skyes pa dang /bud myed dang /gzho nu ma 'tshal zhing phyogs phyogs dag tu ya mchi lags so//glang po che cig gyis shing rta drangs shing //phyogs dag tu yang mchi lagaso
16b6(1): chibs rta dang /rnga mo dang /khyu mchog da nga /ma he dang /ra nga /lug dang /sbrul dang bya dang /skyes pa dang bud myed dang /gzho nu dang /gzho nu ma gcig gyis /shing rta drangs shing phyogs
16b7(1): phyogs dag tu yang mchis lags so//rdzu 'phrul chen po stob bo che /gnod sbyin 'dod dgur maci ba rnams kyi //gzho nu ma de dag 'tshal nas ni //phyogs bzhi dag ni skyong bar bgyid
16b8(1): rgyal po de dag gyi grong khyer dag /sna tshogs kha la rgyu ba ni //'di lta ste kun tu rgyu ba dang /kun tu rgyu ba ma lags pa dang //sa la kun tu rgyu ba ma lags pa dang //gang bcas sa steng kun tu rgyu ba dang /
16b9(1): sbubs can dang /grong khyer sbubs can zhes bgyi ba dag lags so//byang phyogs na 'tsho srab yon zhes bgyi ba yang mchis lags so//nub phyogs na grong khyer gtsang ma gser ldan dang /gos can dang /
16b10(1): gos mchog can dang /pad ma can dang /pad ma bzang po can dang //ngas gzhi can zhes bgyi ba dag kyang mchis lags so//chab rgyun mang po'i gnas gyurd pa//'dzind pa zhes bgyi ba'i
17a1(1): //mtsho mchis te //chab rgyun tha dad de las 'bab //de las dgung chang 'byung bar 'gyur //btsun ba grong khyer u shi ra'i sbubs kyi pha rol na //lus gnas dang ni gnas yangs dang //de bas gnas yangs
17a2(1): rgyun lugs can //rgyun lugs mang dang de bas mang //bar snang kha la bya ltar gda' //btsun ba grong khyer sbubs can gyi pha rold byang phyogs na //'dur sa bya gar can zhes bgyi ba de mchis te //deng ni gnod sbyin rnams 'du zhing gnas lags ste //
17a3(1): rkang myi sdug la chu phyangs dang //'jigs rung lto sbu can dag mchis //de na ljon shing 'bras rtag //rma bya khrung khrung sba snyan dang //khu byug sgra skad chen po dang //shang shang de'u dag sgra 'byind cing //rma bya rnams kyi sgra dag dang //
17a4(1): ko'u kum b+ha dang chu lag dang //bya rnams sna tshogs skad byind cing //me tog sbrang rtsa'i ro la dga' //ku pe ra de glu gar gyis //pad ma can dang lhan cig rtse //btsun pa grong khyer lcang lo can gyi pha
17a5(1): rold na //rgyal bo dga' bo zhes bgyi ba yul 'khor mu khyud na mchi sa lags so //bdag gang na mchis pa'i 'dun sa de ni khyu mchog can zhes bgyi ste //bdag byi mying ni rnam thos kyi bu 
17a6(1): zhes bgyi lags so //de la gsang sngags kyi tshig ni 'di dag lagaso/////tad yA tha /a le mi le /i le mi le /ka ta Ti mu le//hi li ki ta ti mu le //hU du me /
17a7(1): hU dur du me /go'u si gan+d+ha ni /go d+ha ni /o ge mog ke ya swA hA////btsun pa bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar bdag gyis dri za ba rnams dang /dri za ba chen po rnams dang //dri za'i sde
17a8(1): dbon rnams dang dmag dbon rnams kyi mying brjod par bgyi lags so //'di lta ste /'dod myos dga' bas myos pa dang //klu yis myos pa stun dang ni //'phreng bas myos dang sbrang rtsis myos //me tog myos pa sbun
17a9(1): rnams dang //rtag tu myos dang dpal gyis myos //nor gyis myos dang dga' byed dang //ud pa la pad ma zla ba dang //lnga 'dzind dang ni ne tso'i sgra //tum bu ru dang phug lnga dang //gser spyod bu mo gser can dang //
17a10(1): pa ba sangs dang phur bu dang //'gro na ci snyed rgyal po lags //de dag ni bdag gyi sbun lags te //de dag la bdag dri za 'di ni bdag la tshe 'o//dri za 'di ni tha dag myi gtong ngo zhes mchi zhing //

17b4(1): phyag 'tshal bar 'gyur ba 'di nyid //rgya cher lung mnos shing //'don bar bgyid pa de la gal te dri za 'am /dri za mo 'am /dri za rgan po 'am /dri za rgan mo 'am //dri bu pho 'am /dri za bu mo 'am //

18b10(1): dang kra shis dang//mya ngan myed dang mya ngan bral /rab grol dang ni rgyal ba'i bdag //des yangs po dang des bkra dang /yangs pa can dang bkra thub rgyal//gdengs ka tshe dang skar mdog dang /nag po tshe dang lag grol dang //
19a1(1): //ra skyong dang ni 'jog po dang//g.yon phyogs de bzhin nag po dang //sdo bsangs dkar dang grogs pa ni//'gro gnas ci snyed mchis pa lags //de dag ni bdag gyi spun lags te//de bla bdag klu 'di ni

24a1(1): //brgya mtshor rten pa'i lha myin rnams //thams cad nor lhas skrag gyurd cing //mtsho la skyabs su 'du shes pa //thang zangs ris dang rab bka' dang/rab sem byed dang btang bzung dang//stobs ldan
